What we filtered out

Every deal we evaluated and didn't post — with the reason. If the posted price didn't match Amazon, or Amazon already had it cheaper, it lands here instead of in your inbox. Posts we simply didn't get to check are counted, not listed — a skipped post isn't a bad deal.
